# In-Depth System Architecture Specification

The system relies on Docker, Flask, and Gunicorn deployed over an SQLite matrix.

## Deep Convolutional Matrix Mechanics

Equation 0: $Y_{i} = ReLU6(BN(DepthwiseConv(X_{i-1})))$. The inverted residual block ensures a memory footprint constrained to $\mathcal{O}(k^2 \cdot C_{in} \cdot C_{out} / t)$ where expansion ratio $t=6$. This specifically isolates the medicinal leaf veins from the chlorophyll background.
